# Rows above main

One table for every row above main that a landing opens, so the tuning phase starts from a complete list. Compile-time losses are rows too (metric `Ir`; there `main` is the pin). Workers add their rows in their PRs; w-6 merges and de-duplicates. `tools/test_above_main_rows.py` refuses a row without an owner and an exit.

Metric: `clocks` (486 model, `tools/rows/`), `bytes` (object or LEDATA), `Ir` (callgrind or `perf instructions:u`). Delta is parity minus main. A date in `exit` is the day the row is re-measured or closed.

Rate (clock + 3 B per clock-equivalent, min layout, 6502 O1): long_compare_chain +9,221, known_loads_walks +4,436, bit_test +9: worse at the rate, so rows | a select whose branch reads Z or N (`beq`, `bne`, `bmi`, `bpl`) keeps its head move before the compare (a load or a transfer sets N and Z), so with A live the move goes through a register: `ldy #0; cmp #0; beq; ldy #1; tya` where the one-instruction select was `bne; txa; rts`, and in a loop `pha;txa;tay;pla`; carry branches (`bcc`, `bcs`) are not affected (`spill::COMPARE`). The head copy before the compare is the pin's ([X86InstrInfo.cpp#L5640](https://github.com/llvm/llvm-project/blob/d1106deb71cc81016406a1917d0508d2df296266/llvm/lib/Target/X86/X86InstrInfo.cpp#L5640): x86 hoists `MOV32r0` above the compare); the defect for bit_test is the return join `tya; rts`. Early tail duplication does not apply: the pin refuses a return before allocation ([TailDuplicator.cpp#L643](https://github.com/llvm/llvm-project/blob/d1106deb71cc81016406a1917d0508d2df296266/llvm/lib/CodeGen/TailDuplicator.cpp#L643)) and the shared `EarlyTailDuplicate` on the 6502 LIR copies 0 blocks (probe 2026-11-10, 65C02: bit_test 4 -> 4 blocks, memcpy_libcall 22 -> 22, idioms `main` 137 -> 136 and 1 insn). General fix: post-RA duplication of a short return tail into its predecessors, as the pin's late TailDuplicate does (shared `latetaildup` on an allocated LirBody of the 6502 driver, step 5; hand count 17 B = the old size; not shown for long_compare_chain / known_loads_walks); card `~/scratch/handoff/6502-a-bit_test-cause-card-2026-11-10.md` | 6502-c | 2026-11-27 | #8729 |

| 6502-FAS-CLK | frame_address_stack_args (`tests/run/c`) | Os | clocks (code bytes better) | release/next a1d720aafc (6502-llc 569aa432) | perf/6502-trial-bytes 3aa1a3c467 (6502-llc 3216a08f) | 6502 -60 B (1168 -> 1108), 1950 -> 12201 clk (6.3x; 256 pads 1953 -> 12250); 65c02 -61 B, 1946 -> 12165 (256 pads 1949 -> 12214); sim65 PASS | the SZ2 trial of `kernel` into `line` read -99 B and now reads +99 B (calibrated `function_bytes`), so it is refused; `line` keeps its call and its inline cost at `main` goes from -20 to 10 (Os bar 10), so `line` stays a call. Inlined, the chain folded on the constant arguments: `main` was two `report(constant)` calls, now it runs `__mulsi3` three times per `line`. Os is strict bytes; the clock axis is worse. Ruling (oversight 11-11): Os bytes win, the clock loss is the record. A win on both axes would be a trial that keeps the constant folding without the size (a model that sees constant operands of a multiply); best of the others (ext.sh, floor-subtracted, no-print clocks, output checked, 2026-11-11): llvm-mos a40207a371b6 327 cy and 126 B with section GC, which drops the four unreferenced external functions; 1187 B with `--no-gc-sections`, like ours and cc65 (864 B / 16,823 cy); SDCC UNMEASURED (no 6502 harness). Ours Os 1108 B / 12,200 clk (before 1168 B / 1950 clk): 79 B below llvm-mos like for like, 37x its clocks. llvm-mos inlines the chain into main and folds the report arguments, which costs bytes while the externals stay; the byte axis is not behind | 6502-c | review on a bytes-and-clocks win (oversight 11-11: Os stays strict on bytes) | #9138 |

| AM-CZ-1 | x_bubble m32 | O2,O3 | clocks | 74487 | 88533 | +14046 (+18.9%); Os 81513 to 95665 (+17.4%) | the inner loop: main's gcc-style LSR counts it to zero over a biased pointer (6 instructions a trip); the LLVM LSR, pin-equal at the IR, leaves `inc; cmp; jne` with an IV copy (8 a trip; the pin's llc 7). The zero-end formula needs a start of minus the variable bound, with the address sharing that counter: a formula neither the pin nor ours generates (its ICmpZero formulas are `bound - i` and a scale -1); #3547 adds +4.3% to it; 2026-11-10 I1 = m32-owner-table-bytes-2026-11-10-codedata.tsv (x86-m32-bytes, release/next 9c8ce47716): O2/O3 CLOSED (66688 vs main 74487, below main). Os open: 95810 vs main 81513 (+17.5%; gcc 88537, clang 94491). Os cause (greedy-os-causes, harness --hist, dist at release/next a034ebb44e): not the count-to-zero formula (ours is `inc; cmp reg,reg; jne` as gcc's). The inner trip carries a[j+1] into the next trip (GVN load PRE phi): the swap arm (51% of 7,260 trips) ends in a taken `jmp` over the carry copy `mov ebx,ebp` (JMP 3,703 / 11,109 clk vs gcc 359 / 1,077) = 11.1k clk, 11.6% of the cell. Tail-duplicating the 3-instruction latch into the swap arm removes it (+5 B); gcc's copy_bb_p refuses at -Os (grows the code) and the pin's tail-dup size is 1 at optsize, so the byte axis wins and the cell stays. `-fno-tree-pre` is worse (97,006 clk, 171 B) | x86-m32 | 2026-11-20 | #3547, #8905 |

| X87-FIR-1 | x_fir m32 | O1 | clocks | 212662 (best external, gcc) | 230504 | +17842 (+8.4%); inner-loop `imul` order +15.5k of it, offset -6.4k by ours counting to zero | per-address clocks, dist-pch6 1829e01303, 2026-11-10 (`~/scratch/handoff/x_fir-o1-card-2026-11-10.md`): fill loop +8736 (X87-FIR-2), inner loop +9119, outer -400, rest ~+390. Inner: the 486 times `imul r,r/m` by its source operand (10 + max(log2, n)): ours `imul ecx,ebp` has x[i+k] (12 bits, 21.0 clk avg), gcc `imul edx,edi` has coef[k] (18.56); ours has no `cmp` in the loop (count to zero, -6400). The swap is Reassociate's rank order (`mul %x,%coef` -> `mul %coef,%x`): pin `opt -passes=reassociate` on our stage-05 IR does the same and pin llc -O1 on our final IR emits our exact `imul`. Both operands are `movsx` of an i16, so nothing static picks the small multiplier. NO lever: a rule that picks gcc's order is row-shaped; operand order by known bits found 0 corpus sites with both operands bounded and different (128 programs, final O2 IR). Reopens with value knowledge (PGO, parked) | x87 | 2026-11-20 | |

| X87-CORDIC-1 | x_cordic m32 | O1 | clocks | 50252 (gcc; clang 54429) | 50,229 (2026-11-13; was 54,429 before #9040) | -23 clk (below gcc) | CLOSED 2026-11-13: re-measured on the post-#9040 harness at release/next after #9545 (dist stamp 1d2992e224470fba623311c72867e0bcd917441a, i486): x_cordic O1 ours 50,229 clk, 139 B; gcc 50,252 clk (208 B), clang 54,429 clk (145 B): both axes at or under the best of gcc/clang. History: per-address clocks, dist-pch6 1829e01303, run twice from fresh rebuilds, identical (2026-11-10, `~/scratch/handoff/x_cordic-o1-atn-card-2026-11-10.md`): the 12-step loop is equal (ours 47192 clk, gcc 47208: our `mov`+`op` = gcc's folded `op r,[m]`, 2 clk), the whole gap is the outer loop. (a) 3800: `imul eax,ecx,12Ch` every iteration; gcc has a stride-300 IV (`add edi,12Ch`) and its lea/add chain only once in the prologue; `i*300+C` is not reduced because `i` is also the exit compare. Pin llc -O1/-O2 on our final IR emits the same imul, so it is the pin's LSR on this loop (owner w12 / m32-o1-small POC: -3200 clk at +5 B, bar slack 6). (b) 400: the atn load common to both arms is hoisted before the branch (SimplifyCFG late slot, `hoistCommonCodeFromSuccessors`; the pin does the same), so neither arm folds it, the inner loop needs a 7th register and the outer loop spills sum and i (4 stack moves an iteration against 2). Probe with one load per arm: -397 clk, -8 B code, spills 2 -> 1. Closed by #8861 (X87-FOLD-HOIST): an x86 `isProfitableToHoist` (the hook the pin's AArch64 and PowerPC override), 54429 -> 54032 clk. the 977 left after the POC = 600 (its chain) + 400 (spills) - 23. | X87-IIR-1 | x_iir m32 | O1 | clocks | 60034 (gcc; clang 130528) | 130528 (= clang, same code) | +70494 (+117%) | three `imul` an iteration (`imul ebx,esi,-700`, the LCG `imul edx,19660Dh`, `imul edi,esi,640h`) are 72 of the 87 clk an iteration; gcc turns them into lea/shl/add chains (31 insns, 40 clk an iteration, 168 B against ours 111 B). The pin's llc on the same IR emits the same imuls (`iir-pin/pin.O1.s`). A chain is bytes-neutral only for an imm32 imul and a 2-instruction chain (c = 3, 5 or 9 times 2^k, c > 127): none of x_iir's constants. O1 is judged per axis, so the chain's extra bytes (+57 B) are not allowed; our bytes are 111 against clang 122 (-57 B against gcc). | X87-CORDIC-2 | x_cordic m32 | O3 | clocks + 0.312 x bytes | 26977 (gcc: 26743 clk, 749 B; clang 45252 clk, 654 B) | 26743 clk / 772+4 B (gcc13; clang 45252 clk) | 27424 clk / 763+4 B (release/next 3961e1c7f1) | +681 clk, +678 at 0.312 x B (+2.5%) | 2026-11-12 re-measured, dist cc 50581c380661 / llc e2937703246d, `VSGCC_LD=64`: ours 20768 instrs / 601 memory operands, gcc 20126 / 202. The phi fold is closed (PHICONST, PHICONST-2): no phi with constants and one variable incoming is left in the unrolled steps, and the pin's `foldOpIntoPhi` (L1980) clones for one non-simplifying incoming only for a one-use phi, which our port does. The residue is the outer loop's `i*300` IV spill: 3 memory operands an iteration (`mov [esp],ebp`, `mov ebp,[esp]`, `cmp [esp+24],esi`) against gcc's 1, 399 of the 642 extra instructions and about the whole clock gap; cause (m64-c, 2026-11-12, read against gcc 13 and the pin's i386 -O3 listings): InstCombine (ours and the pin) canonicalizes `i*300-30000 >= 0` to `i u> 99`, so `i` stays a second live IV beside `z0 = i*300-30000` and the unrolled body spills `z0`. gcc keeps the compare on `z0` and its ivopts folds the exit test onto it with a precomputed limit (1 IV, 1 memory operand). The pin spills the same 3 slots as ours: parity with the pin, not an LSR or regalloc defect; closing it is a gcc-ivopts compare-use elimination, beyond the pin. Not LSR-OUTER, not a fold. Unlanded: a binop of two multi-use phis in another block (pin `foldBinopWithPhiOperands` :2215, one-use and same-block TODOs), about 2 instructions an iteration on one path. | ARM64-OS-ROT-1 | recsum / recfloat / sieve arm64 (llrm-cc) | Os | kernel bytes | gcc 40 / 64 / 152 (clang 156 / 84 / 184; clang IR + pin llc 72 / 84 / 184) | 48 / 72 / 164 | +8 (+20%) / +8 (+12.5%) / +12 (+7.9%) against the best of gcc and clang | a gap to gcc, not to the pin (ours is smaller than clang and the pin on all three). Ours rotates, as the pin does (LoopRotation.cpp#L79-84: threshold 16 at -Os); the guard leaves a second exit block `mov x0, xzr; ret` beside the loop `ret` (+2 instructions in recsum); gcc keeps the unrotated `cbz` at the top and one `ret` (tree-ssa-loop-ch copies a header at -Os only when code does not grow). Tried: the size rotation header limit and exit pricing (RS1): 14 other Os cells worse. Measured 2026-11-09T21:28Z, dist = llrm-llc 552babcff7f0 (48ff8a3649 + hooks build base), gcc 15 and clang from build2.sh | w15 | 2026-11-24: a general growth test over all loops from gcc's tree-ssa-loop-ch size rule (GPL optional crate), A/B on the whole arm64 corpus; closed only by a fix or oversight's ruling | |
| ARM64-OS-1 | recpow arm64 (llrm-cc, -fomit-frame-pointer) | Os | kernel bytes | gcc 184 (clang 308, clang IR + pin llc 308) | 192 | +8 (+4.3%) against gcc | a gap to gcc, not to the pin. Allocator remat across a call: `power` (92 B against gcc's 84) holds the constant 30011 in callee-saved x20 across the recursive call (32 B frame, second saved pair); the pin llc on the same loop makes it in the preheader and again after `bl` (frame 16 B, no x20). The prepass is not the lever: dropping the `optsize` clause of `prepass::cse` removes x20 but moves recpow only 192 -> 188 B and makes 9 Os cells worse (jpeg_fixed +20, lru +16, stft_single +24; w15's 36-program Os A/B), so the carve-out stays. Not the allocator (2026-11-10): the pin's greedy given the merged vreg also takes x20 (pin llc -start-after=machine-cse on our IR, the two 30011 defs hand-merged: same `stp x20, x19` frame); the pin avoids it only by never merging them. A narrower carve-out, merging a cheap def at -Os unless a call lies on a path def -> use, closes recpow (192 -> 188) but makes jpeg_fixed +24, jpeg_single +8, stft_fixed +12, stft_single +24 (36-program -Os A/B), so a lever needs register-pressure knowledge (a CSR already in use), beyond the pin. Repro, IR, pin output and the parked fail-first (`alim/arm64-os1-const-across-call`, `constant_across_call.rs`): handoff/arm64-judge-gaps-cards-2026-11-09.md (6) | w15 (prepass CSE, SD530) | 2026-11-30 | |

Ours equals the pin (mandel) or is smaller. | ARM64-OS-5 | switch / matmul arm64 (llrm-cc, -fomit-frame-pointer) | Os | kernel bytes | gcc 252 / 280 (clang 320 / 496; pin 320 / 304) | 256 / 288 | +4 (+1.6%) / +8 (+2.9%) against gcc | gaps to gcc, ours below the pin. lru left the row 2026-11-09: gcc built -fPIE (ours' default) is 356 B, ours 356 B, same. switch (owner w15, exit 2026-11-30): a gap to gcc, beyond the pin, not a missing pass. gcc ends the 7-case compare chain of `route` with `cmp x1, 0; csinc x1, x14, xzr, eq` (its own if-conversion of the last case into the default, 1 = xzr + 1); ours keeps `cmp; b.ne default; mov w, #N; b join`. The pin does not convert it either: pin llc on our post-mid-end IR with -stress-early-ifcvt, and with -mcpu=apple-m1 / cortex-a76, gives identical .text (0x168) and no csel/csinc/ccmp (`SSAIfConv::canConvertIf` needs a 2-predecessor tail and the join has many); bytes ours 256, gcc 252, pin llc 320. Lever (gcc side): a select-forming step for single-case leaves of the lowered compare tree; card by 2026-11-14 only if it is general, else the row stays recorded. matmul (owner the arm64 judge session, exit 2026-11-30): `bench_matmul` is 72 instrs ours against gcc's 70 (+8 B). Not an induction-variable widening (ours' IVs are already 64-bit; ldrsh x/w and madd/smaddl are the same size). The cost is +4 moves (copies of loop-invariant bases into per-loop pointer registers before the loops, `orr x13, xzr, x10`), offset by 2 fewer branches; the lever is machine-level coalescing, not found at IR level. Pin llc (-O2, pic, also apple-m1) on our post-mid-end IR (`LLRM_MIR_STAGES`, last stage) is 76 instrs = 304 B, 16 B above ours, so no pin pass does better on this IR; the pin's own clang flow is 304 B too. Cause of the 8 B to gcc, by region: init nest +2 instrs (ours recomputes `seed+1+3i` with two adds per outer iteration and keeps a third IV for `i`; gcc steps `w0 += 3`), the rest are the same counts; gcc's IVopts choice, not a missing translation. Lever: the `LsrModel` A/B (ARM64-OS-3, after w12's trait). Measured on release/next a5a8f0d252 dist, 2026-11-10: ours 288 B, gcc 280, clang 496. Cause card 2026-11-09 | w15 (switch); llrm-arm64-judge-gaps (matmul) | switch 2026-11-30; matmul 2026-11-30 | |
| ARM64-QUEENS-O2 | queens arm64 (llrm-cc, -fomit-frame-pointer) | O2 | kernel instrs / bytes | gcc 114,028 i / 228 B (clang 119,909 i / 236 B) | 118,830 i / 216 B | +4,802 i (+4.2%) against gcc, net +4.2%; ours below clang | gcc threads the failing-path back edge of `place`'s column loop past the loop-invariant entry guard of the inlined `safe` loop (`cmp row,#1; b.lt`, 2 instrs x 3,584 per run); the hot inner loop is identical (10 instrs per iteration both). The pin does not: pin opt -passes=jump-threading on our final IR leaves `place` byte-identical (opt 810951efee00, llc 7b90c2447c1a) and clang's own -O2 is 119,909. Our O2 list has jumpthreading@668 and @809 (`pipeline.rs`). No pin pass, so DEVIATE candidate: gcc's dominator/ranger threading | llrm-arm64-judge-gaps | 2026-11-30 | |
| ARM64-RECGCD-O2 | recgcd arm64 (llrm-cc, -fomit-frame-pointer) | O2 | kernel instrs / bytes | gcc 17,314 i / 132 B (clang 17,611 i / 120 B) | 17,611 i / 112 B | +297 i (+1.7%) against gcc, equal to clang | per-pc counts (stamped tip ba096315a2): both sides run the same blocks except the first `gcd(i*7+3, i*5+2)` loop. gcc enters it past the loop-top copy `mov w2,w1` (899 runs against 1,199 for the other four instrs) and puts its two start copies in the preheader; ours copies at the top every pass (5-instr loop in ours, gcc and pin llc). Net +297 instrs, not 300 free copies: gcc pays extra IV registers. Cause shown (stamped tip ba096315a2; local diagnostics in `why_left` and `JoinVals::map_values`, not committed, output ~/scratch/jg-why.out and jg-why2.out): `LLRM_COALESCE_WHY=1` leaves 6 copies, all "conflict (deferred)"; the loop-top copy is block 4 `v19 <- v42` (id 23). Its join fails in `map_values` on a real overlap, not a coalescer quirk: v19 (the phi value `a`, also the loop's live-out) is live over [432r,816r); v42 (the phi temp of `b`) has the back-edge copy `v42 <- v43` defined at 512r inside that range (v42: [384r,416B) [416B,432r) [512r,576B)). The back-edge copy sits before the latch branch, so it executes on the exit path while v19 is still live: v19 and v42 interfere. The pin does not split that edge either: PHIElimination.cpp#L827-832 skips loop back edges ("small out-of-line blocks into the loop ... bad for code placement"; https://github.com/llvm/llvm-project/blob/d1106deb71cc81016406a1917d0508d2df296266/llvm/lib/CodeGen/PHIElimination.cpp#L827), and pin llc on our final IR keeps the copy (17,611 executed, as pin clang). gcc keeps the copy on the back edge (`cbnz w1, 2c` to `2c: mov w2,w1`, 899 runs) and enters the loop past it. So no pin pass reaches gcc's; the lever would be splitting a back edge whose phi source is live-out, which reverses the pin's choice: a DEVIATE, to be priced against code placement. Not shown: whether w15's fib O2 "conflict (deferred)" (multi-def argument vreg) is the same cause; this overlap is a different mechanism, so do not assume it | llrm-arm64-judge-gaps | 2026-11-30 | |

| M64-BIND-1 | tail_calls (m64 -fPIE / -fno-pic / unflagged); the 40 m64 cells #8696 shortened | O1,O2,O3,Os,Oz | R_X86_64_PLT32 on a call or jump to a same-object global (and 3 B per shortened jump) | pin 25 (clang -fPIE 25) | 9 | -16 relocations, below the pin (#8696); gcc -fPIE (-fno-inline) 26 / 19 / 19 / 19 at O1 / O2 / O3 / Os | DEVIATE 1 in objbuild.rs. The pin's MC resolves a PC-relative reference only to STB_LOCAL (ELFObjectWriter.cpp L1391), so it leaves every global to the linker even when a PIE or static link cannot interpose it; ours binds a definition no other module can replace (`masm::Module::preemptible` lists the ones that can: weak, and default-visibility at -fPIC) and gcc/gas do the same for an executable. #8696 binds the resolved sibling tails (25 -> 9 at -fPIE). #8735 keeps PLT32 for what another module can replace: tail_calls -O2 -fPIC 9 -> 25, 44 programs 157 -> 169 PLT32, -fPIE 9 / 157 unchanged (same binaries, 8dace0f22 vs 2de7ad85af); bytes at -fPIE: 0 of 176 cells moved. At -fPIC ours is the pin's: 25 relocations, gcc -fno-inline 25, clang 25 (same flags). Checked end to end on x86_64 and x86_32 against the pin and gcc: weak and, at -fPIC, default-visibility globals keep PLT32; hidden and static bind | x86-m64 | 2026-12-10, review; leaves when the bar is no longer gcc or the pin binds locally | #8696, #8735 |

Cause: x86-64 `timings.times` is PROVISIONAL Skylake-class figures (its header says so); a back edge is priced ~3.8 clk a trip. Pricing the nest changes no byte (tried). The shipped imuls are 32 x `x * 1664525` (one per function) and three small ones (125, 1000, 100); the pin takes the rest. Real chains: 1664525 9 clk / 23 B for the fewest-clock and the fewest-byte ranking alike (imul 6 B / 31 clk), 125 / 1000 / 100 at 9-12 B. Lower bound on any chain (mulchains.py: none of <= 5 ops; with >= 6 ops and one 3-byte op the growth cannot reach 2^20.7): >= 14 B / >= 6 clk, +8 B and <= 25 clk saved per trip. The clock bar needs 144,697 clk: <= 5,000 per function from the 1664525 site, so >= 28 functions even with the three small sites chained (+18 B): >= +242 B against +219 B of slack. Dominance rule (a chain only where no longer and faster): changed no object over 6,000 `int` and `short` constants at -O1 and -Os; the INL-O1 rate (156 mclk/B) reproduces the fewest-clock chains (x_fir 188 -> 221 B, above both bars) | x86-m32-bytes | 2026-11-17 | |

| M32-O1-ADLER | x_adler x86-m32 | O1 | clocks | gcc 47,974 clk (160 B; clang 57,094 clk, 115 B) | 57,094 clk, 111 B | +9,120 clk (+19%) | equal to clang to the clock (the pin's code). `--hist`: ours 13,526 `mov` against gcc's 3,005: asm diffed 2026-11-13: ours `mov edx,eax; sub edx,0xfff1; jae L; mov edx,eax; L:` (4 insns when no subtract is needed, the common case), gcc `cmp edx,0xfff0; jbe L; sub edx,0xfff1; L:` (2). The IR input has a branch around the `sub`; SimplifyCFG `speculativelyExecuteBB`/`foldTwoEntryPHINode` makes it `select(a > 65520, a - 65521, a)` and the i486 (no CMOV) lowers the select to a branch diamond with the `sub` before it (EmitLoweredSelect); the pin's clang -march=i486 emits the same code. A/Bs on 121 programs x O1/Os/O2/O3, all refused: (a) no one-armed speculation without CMOV: x_adler unchanged, counted 63/85/37/37 -> 63/85/37/38; (b) no two-entry fold where a select is a branch: Os 85 -> 86 (+x_adler, x_primes; -sfm16_float), O1 63 -> 68 (-x_crc32 +22,535 clk +19 B), O2 37 -> 38 and O3 37 -> 39 (-x_crc32); (c) fold only where the select lowers branchless (two constants, or `x op K` against `x`): O1 65 -> 66 (+x_collatz), Os 85 -> 85, O2/O3 37 -> 36 (-x_crc32 +13,963 clk +116 B) and x_adler unchanged. x_crc32's select (a 50/50 xor) wants the fold and x_adler's (rare) wants the branch; with no profile the two differ only by branch probability, which is SelectOptimize's `isSelectHighlyPredictable` (ported, unwired: the pin's llc disables it, TargetPassConfig.cpp:278), not by shape. | llrm-target-x86-m32-b | 2026-11-24 | - |

## Recorded, not worse than the bar

No owner or exit: a cell under the best external at its judging metric is not a worse cell. It reopens if the bar moves under ours.

- **RECPOW-M64-OS** (2026-11-13): recpow x86_64-linux Os, main 65098fd950 `.text` 217 B -> 189 B (-28 B). A first A/B read +4 B because its `size` column counted `.eh_frame` (120 -> 152 B: main inlined `power` into one function, the tip keeps it with its own FDE); the bar is code bytes. The count-down loop is the rotation trial (kept: -3 B). Instrument fixed: the A/B takes `.text` only.
- **WASM-OS-JPEG-CLAMP-8669** (#8669): jpeg_fixed wasm32 Os, 21318 B / 51836453 instrs -> 21320 B / 51983909 (+2 B, +147456 instrs). 80 B under the best external (the pin's clang, 21400 B / 51291412); instrs are an instrs-only regression, unjudged at Os. `clamp` is a call whose body is now the pin's 12-instruction form (11 before), one more instruction on each of 3 x 49152 calls. The pin inlines it (cost -25, threshold 75); SZ1/D11 (`opt_size_inline_by_code_size`, own row and exit) refuses it. With the pin's rule: 21381 B / 51291418 instrs, so SZ1 holds 61 B for +692491 instrs. After #8749 (negative-cost yield, `0adcd1f3cf`): 21328 B / 51365895 instrs (llrm-target-wasm's fix dist, not yet the landed tree; 72 B under the pin, +74477 instrs over its 51291412); jpeg_single Os 21339 B / 47851527 against the pin's 21423 B / 47777044.

## D-row: expression-carrying debug records and `salvageDebugInfo` (AM-DBG-1)

Design only; no code before the coordinator's go.

**What the pin keeps.** `observed.c` at -O2 -g through clang-20 leaves `#dbg_value` records among the code, each with a `DIExpression`: `plus_uconst`, `constu N; mul|shl; stack_value`, `LLVM_fragment` for the pieces of `v`, `p`, `q`, and `DIArgList` for a value of several operands. When a pass deletes or folds an instruction, `salvageDebugInfo` (Utils/Local.cpp, `salvageDebugInfoImpl`) rewrites the records that used it as an expression over its operands (an add of a constant becomes `plus_uconst`, a shl `constu; shl`, a cast `convert`) instead of killing them. Result on this fixture: 18 values read like -O0, none wrong.

**Ours.** `DebugWhat` (llrm-mir `module.rs`) is `Value(operand)`, `Piece`, `GonePiece`, `Gone`, `Declare`: a bare value, no expression. `Function::erase` of a value's last definition ends in `Gone`. So `c = n * 6` folded into `mul %0, 6` is lost with its variable. The output side already writes constants and pieces (`NoteValue`, `DW_OP_piece`).

**Minimal slice that revives a, b, c, i** (three PRs):
1. MIR: `DebugWhat::Value` gains `expr: Vec<ExprOp>` (`PlusUconst`, `Constu`, `Mul`, `Shl`, `Plus`, `StackValue`); printer, parser and verifier read it. Pieces unchanged.
2. Salvage: a `Function::salvage(inst)` for single-operand cases (add/sub/mul/shl by constant, zext/sext/trunc), called where InstCombine, IndVars, LoopDeletion and SimplifyCFG erase or replace a value (the pin's call sites); everything else stays `Gone`. No `DIArgList` in this slice.
3. Backend: `NoteValue::Expr(vreg, ops)` in isel `noted`, and the DWARF writer emits `DW_OP_bregN 0; ops; DW_OP_stack_value` (CodeView keeps the plain value).

**Cost** (estimate, not measured): about 700 lines and 3 PRs; compile time unchanged without `-g` (records exist only then); objects identical with and without `-g` (the `g_leaves_*` tests guard it). Done when `a_variable_reads_the_same_at_o2_as_at_o0...` passes at floor 18 and loses its `ignore`.

## m68k onto `machine_with` (owner m68k-lsr-addressing, ruling 2026-11-09; closes JT-O1-1..3)

Estimates, not measurements; CSE comes through the shared flow. Gap card: `~/scratch/handoff/m68k-onto-machine-with-gaps-2026-11-09.md`. Every step: x86 md5 identity, m68k A/B bytes and clocks (68000/68020, O1/O2/Os), compile time linear at 600 and 1200 functions.

- 2026-11-09 (done): pre-phi `MFunction` to `LirBody` and back (null stage equal on 18 functions x 2 CPUs); the shared `precse` and `MachineLICM` run on it, both off. `machine_with` builds for m68k.
- 2026-11-09 attribution: hosted CSE and the pin's direct `CopyFromReg` are one effect (68000 O2 +394 B, 16 of 20 worse cells shared): the pin's MIR shape on private passes tuned on copies (shiftadd, addrroles, loopinc, allocator hints). CSE stays off until they are shape-robust or retired by step 4.
- 2026-11-10 (done): the converter makes each physical write its own pinned value (`4f9cee96bf`, m68k-machine-with): 20 of 20 fixture functions pass `flow::checked`; `from_ssa` for new copies and blocks is next.
- JT-O1-1..3 against the external bar (sieve -O1, whole-object .text, gcc 15.2.0 / clang 20.1.8, 2026-11-10): bytes ok (ours 164/154/154 against gcc 166/162/162); clocks worse (68000 289112 against 189350, +53%; 68020 +10.8%; 68040 not priced), cause `i*i` before the `i<32` guard, owner llrm-target-m68k. The rows stay open until the main-column difference is fixed.
- 2026-11-12 20:00Z: report (this list, per-pass losses, the verdicts).
- 2026-11-18: step 4a: shared PhiElimination and TwoAddress hosted (`from_ssa` takes new copies, renamed defs, new vregs); private `eliminate_phis_with`, `fold_reserved_copies`, `commute_tied`, `two_address`, `machine_sink` retired; m68k classes, rules and the omitted-phase list from the target description.
- 2026-11-24: step 4b: the copy-shape cells re-measured (CSE and `direct_copies` on); the private passes that still lose a cell are made shape-robust or replaced. Part of the cost is decided before phi elimination: `addrroles`/`addrarith` put a value in a data register once its copy is gone (nbody), so step 4a does not retire it; the class choice moves to the allocator, as in the pin. The omitted-phase list stays open (x86-only phases inert is unproven: no frame, calls, rules or verifier in the test).
- 2026-11-30: post-RA copyprop, branch folding, block placement shared.
- 2026-12-04: frame/epilogue from `FrameRegisters`; replaced private passes deleted; JT-O1-1..3 closed.

Files, one writer each (merge direction one way: m68k-machine-with branches off `alim/m68k-machine-with` b654939e04):
- m68k-lsr-addressing: this section, the first-running-step report (2026-11-12 20:00Z), the A/B scripts (`~/scratch/handoff/m68k-mw/`); no code from b654939e04 on.
- m68k-machine-with: step 3 and on: `llrm-spike-mf/src/tolir.rs`, `llrm-codegen/src/backend/precse.rs`, `llrm-m68k/src/{mfunc,regalloc,compile}.rs`, their tests.

## N/A cells (not like)

huge, lru and textfill (bench) do not compile at m68k on any CPU or level: DOS-only sources (`__huge`, `far`, the `SCREEN` macro). They are N/A in sweeps, not errors, and not counted in the cell totals (438 cells = 420 compiled + 18 N/A).

Where: +1200 PUSH and +1200 POP (1.5 per call) and +1697 MOV. Read from the objects on the same IR: the pin saves 3 registers in __gedf2 and 3 (+1 slot) in __cmpdf2, one exit each, no shrink-wrapping; ours saves 4 and 3, one exit; gcc15 pushes 2 and returns through several exits. |  M32-OS-ROT-CLK | x_prefix, x_base64, fib, x_crc32, x_radix, x_jpeg_fixed, x_idioms, sfm16_dbg (m32) | Os | clocks (bytes strict) | release/next e0c889cb7b: x_prefix 50,427 clk / 163 B | ROTATION-TRIAL: 58,870 clk / 151 B | clocks vs best of gcc13/gcc15/clang: x_prefix -0.0% -> +16.7%, x_base64 -0.9% -> +7.1%, fib +1.2% -> +9.2%, x_crc32 +1.6% -> +4.9%, x_radix +12.7% -> +17.9%, x_jpeg_fixed +2.5% -> +3.8%, sfm16_dbg +3.6% -> +6.8%, x_idioms +4.4% -> +5.0% | DEVIATE ROTATION-TRIAL keeps gcc's unrotated shape where it is smaller; the best clock in 7 of 8 cells is clang's rotated code (more bytes: x_prefix clang 182 B / 50,431 clk, gcc 134 B / 58,238, ours 151 B / 58,870). Against gcc (13 and 15 agree) ours is behind on clocks in three cells only: x_prefix +1.1%, fib +3.0%, sfm16_dbg +6.8% (best there is gcc); ahead in x_radix -1.2%, x_base64 -31%, x_idioms -38%, x_crc32 -26%, x_jpeg_fixed -46..-39%. So the first differing decision is loop rotation (the trial picks gcc's side of a bytes/clocks pair where bytes are strict at Os); the byte gap to gcc left (x_prefix +17 B: a pointer IV plus a separate count-down IV, `sub esi,1; jb` + `add edi,4`, where gcc keeps one scaled index; `-fno-lsr` -4 B only) is LSR, w12's LG3 row. sfm16_dbg (+405 clk from the trial, base already +3.6% behind gcc): cause unread. fib (+3.0% vs gcc, 10,946 clk = one extra instruction per call, read from harness --hist): the loop-exit sum is a tied `add esi,edi; mov eax,esi` where gcc writes `lea eax,[ebx+esi]`. The pin does not convert it either: TwoAddressInstructionPass converts at L1402 only if the tied source is not killed or `isProfitableToConv3Addr` holds (L784: `SrcRegMap[regB]` set by a COPY in the same block); the source `n` is a loop phi, lowered to copies in the loop blocks, so `SrcRegMap` has no entry in the exit block and `ToRegA` ($eax) alone does not suffice. `codegen/twoaddress.rs` translates the same condition. Not a translation bug; the gap is the unrotated loop's phi-fed exit sum 2026-11-12 A/B on tip f379c28e91 (m32 -Os, 121 programs, one build each), all refused: (a) SZ4 header limit back to the pin's 16: 2 programs change, both worse (x_rle +15 B / +297 clk, x_hash +4 B / +2,367 clk), counted 85 -> 84; (b) RS1 + ROTATION-TRIAL + ROTATION-HOT off, the pin's `LoopRotate` alone: counted 85 -> 88 (gained fib, x_fib, floats, recchop, x_list; lost nbody 383 B vs bar 382 and x_rle 202 vs 200), recfloat +6,999 clk, x_hash +2,367; (c) ROTATION-TRIAL alone off: 85 -> 85 (gained fib, x_fib, floats; lost nbody, quicksort, x_isqrt +49,926 clk). Rotating buys clocks for 5..16 B a cell and the byte bars are met by 1..2 B in the cells it loses: strict bytes vs strict clocks, not a pass to translate. | greedy-os-causes | 2026-12-05 | #9141  |
| M32-SFM16-OS | sfm16_dbg x86_32-wat | Os | code bytes | not stated (main 65098fd950 not re-taken here) | dist-G 294a7ec6d4: 1987 B code (+17,652 data) / 13,728 clk | gcc 13 1837 B code (+17,668) / 12,855 clk: +150 B code, +134 B total (WORSE, Os is bytes); clang 3016 B / 20,457 clk: ours is -1029 B | 2026-11-11 cause card, harness vsgcc `--hist`, per-function `nm -S` of `llrmElfOs` vs `gccOs` (same flags): bytes before the rotation trial are the same (19,640 -> 19,639 B), so the trial is not the cause; its +405 clk is M32-OS-ROT-CLK. The gap is diffuse: 14 of 27 functions are bigger (+2..+58: __divsf3 +58, add_bits +55, sf32_pack +16, bench_sfm16_dbg +12, sf_clz32 +9, __floatsisf +8, cmp_bits +7), none smaller by more than 10 (__mulsf3 -10). Instruction mix, ours - gcc: mov +30, pop +18 (`pop ecx` for `add esp,4` is a win), push +15, or +15, jae +14 (jbe -7), xor +10, setcc +15 (21 vs 6), and +7; ret count equal (19), calls 22 vs 24. Excluded: (1) the pin: its pipeline on the same C gives 3,034 B (clang 3,016), and `llc -O2` of the pin on OUR final IR gives 2,131 B text, 144 B more than ours, so the backend is not the loser; (2) the select/branch choice is priced by `sizes::cmp_sel(.., cmov)` already (no cmov at i486), the pin makes 85 selects here, ours 32 (gcc's -Os keeps branches where we keep select/setcc/or chains: add_bits block 18 `or.cond1`/`select` pairs become `setne bl; setne bh; or bh,bl; sete cl; or cl,bh; jne`). Examples read: add_bits spills both arguments at entry (`mov [esp],edx; mov [esp+4],ecx`, frame 0x1c vs gcc 0xc) where gcc keeps them in ebx/ebp; sf32_pack keeps the constant 1 (`xor edi,edi; inc edi`) in a callee-saved register across `sf_shr_sticky32` (push/pop edi and esi, +8 B) where gcc re-materialises `mov ecx,1`; the pin's llc does the same (`xor esi,esi; inc esi` live across the call), so ours matches the pin. The pin prices a first callee-saved use (`X86RegisterInfo::getCSRFirstUseCost` = 2, push+pop; translated in `greedy/targetinfo.rs`) and still keeps it; the cost is in entry-frequency units, not a byte price, which is where gcc differs. Not yet read: __divsf3, the rest of add_bits. Candidates, general: a byte price for the callee-saved first use at Os in the allocator (a DEVIATE of the pin's frequency-unit cost), and the byte price of a select/setcc chain against the branches it replaces; neither tried, so no fix yet | greedy-os-causes | 2026-11-27 | none |

| Z80-SIZE-DIVIDE (DEVIATE z80-callinline-price) | copy_runs, loop_interchange, shift_int64_variable, data_int64 (z80) | Os, Oz | bytes (vs the tip; clocks fall) | tip d37cb3eade: 767 / 882 / 872 / 505 B; 729429 / 23321577 / 12343500 / 105474 T | this PR: 953 / 1068 / 1021 / 742 B; 204454 / 9655087 / 6173004 / 19038 T | +186 / +186 / +149 / +237 B; -524975 / -13666490 / -6170496 / -86436 T | a size-tuned function with a long divide takes the speed routine where the module's smaller relative regret says so (`__s_*` is 6.1k T a 32-bit divide and 99k T a 64-bit one, 4-9x the speed routine, for 76-179 B fewer). All four are ahead of the best SDCC on both axes (1518 B / 541410 T; 1237 B / 22553834 T; 1438 B / 11675066 T; 982 B / 70515 T). Typical operand lengths are assumed (24-bit dividend, 8-bit divisor; 32-bit dividend for 64-bit): a program of full-width dividends by small divisors pays more than priced. A function with a multiply or fill alternative keeps the size divide (one switch per function). Policy: -Os/-Oz take the form with the smaller relative regret (bytes against clocks, #9381), not bytes alone; this is that policy, not an unexplained growth | llrm-target-z80-c | 2026-11-24: price each divide site by its operands' known range; independent alternatives per axis | this PR |

| Z80-SHIFT-UNTIL | shift_until_idioms (`tests/run/c`) | O1, O2, O3, Os, Oz | code bytes, clocks | SDCC best-size-ma 1155 B / 677582 T (O1) | 2026-11-12 on 583a529f1e: O2 1159 B / 747228 T, O3 1357 B / 740732 T (O1 786358 T after #9347) | O2 +4 B / +69646 T; O3 +202 B / +63150 T (the bar's 677582 T is the sweep's best SDCC variant; my profile of sdcc-speed-ma is 694.5k) | allocator, per iteration of `offset_count` (~190 T over SDCC): (1) the long counter's high word is stored at the loop head and reloaded at the latch (the interval is split around the inline shift loop that clobbers d, e, h, l, b, not spilled everywhere), ~60 T against SDCC's `inc d(ix)`; (2) the long operand is reloaded as four bytes where SDCC keeps its high word on the stack (`pop de; push de`), ~17 T; (3) the loop condition is computed, stored (`ld -1(ix), a`) and reloaded because the carry step of the counter runs between the test and the branch and clobbers a and the flags (SDCC branches first, steps after), ~42 T; (4) a call to `__ashlsi3` where SDCC inlines the loop. A tied-RMW fold of the step (#9371 is the printer's) has no site left in the O2 corpus: all remaining spilled steps are read again (compare with 0 or 1, carry into the next word). The lever is the choice between splitting and spilling the counter, or branching before the step: allocator (greedy) work. 2026-11-12: priced by hand (asm edited, outputs identical): the exit test before the 32-bit increment plus the high word incremented in place in its slot = 701.8k T (-45.5k, -6.1%); that is the target. Neither piece alone works (an in-place step before the test makes the exit path read the new word), and the sink of the increment past the test is blocked for every fixed-register `Copy Phys / op / Copy v` chain (the pin's own FindSuccToSinkTo refuses them), scoped in ~/scratch/handoff/z80-fixed-reg-chains-vs-sink-2026-11-12.md: virtual-register forms before sink (isel / two_address) | z80 (llrm-target-z80-b; isel session for the virtual forms) | 2026-12-15 | |

The pin's x86 memcpy lowering picks the `rep` width from the alignment (2 -> `movsw`); clang's libc call copies by dword. A dword copy of a 2-aligned block is a hardware misalignment penalty the harness may not price: unmeasured, so no patch. 2026-11-13, tip 8a3bf383d1: +24k clk and +27 B over 23b6d127fd (O1 and O2 +24k clk, +20 B, still counted; O3 unchanged) from #9570 (memset.pattern only where the target has memset_pattern16): the 80-word `store i16 1824` fill loop is a loop again (`--hist` JNE 2,050 -> 6,050, MOV +4,000, ADD +4,000), pin-faithful, no revert. | x86-m32 | 2026-11-24 | this PR |

| M16-CLZ-SELECT-COPY | bitcount_u32 (`tests/run/c`, x86_16-wat `-march=i386`) | O1, O2, O3, Os | code bytes | release/next 7bd8768f35 (llc 7476f262171f): 246 B (O1/O2/O3), 244 B (Os) | #9673 head (llc d5165b83a830): 247 B, 245 B | +1 B at 4 cells; m64 -2 B (bitcount_u64), m32 -3 B (bitcount_u32), 0 larger | no bar exists (no bcc/tc/tcpp column for this file). With the pin's zero-poison price the select `x ? clz(x) : 16` folds to `ctlz(x, false)` as the pin does at i386/i486 (pin opt); the pin then despeculates it to test/je/bsr/xor and tail-duplicates the phi+ret block (pin llc i386: `test; je; bsr; xor; ret; mov $16; ret`). Ours keeps one `retf` and hoists `mov ax, 32` into the entry block, so the argument is copied off `ax` (`mov cx, ax`). Missing piece: DEVIATE ET1 in `backend/earlytaildup.rs` (a tail with a phi is not duplicated; `processPHI`, `updateSuccessorsPHIs`, SSA update not ported) | llrm-greedy-anyisa | 2026-11-24 | #9673 |
